GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes\n\n### Node Types Introduced / Touched (no mcorch_nodes do Supabase)\n\n| node_type | Pattern | Created by | Embedding Source |\n|-----------|---------|------------|-----------------|\n| `observation` | `openclaw-audit-vN`, `pantheon-config-audit-<date>`, `memory-wiki-population-<date>` | claw-master + manual seal | claw-master report content |\n| `documentation_suite` | `bok-scribe-seal-openclaw-v1` (esta seal) | `scripts/seed-openclaw-bok-seal.ts` | suite description |\n| `handoff` | `session-handoff-<date>-openclaw-*` | session seal protocol | handoff narrative |\n| `markdown_file` | `pantheon-role-matrix.md`, `openclaw-self-heal-runbook.md`, etc. | autoembed trigger | file content |\n\n> Este m\u00f3dulo **n\u00e3o introduz tabelas Supabase novas** \u2014 todo dado runtime fica em filesystem local + SQLite per-agente; apenas observability flui para `infra_health_logs`.\n\n### Edge Types Introduced / Touched\n\n| Edge relationship | Source | Target | Metadata |\n|-------------------|--------|--------|----------|\n| `DERIVES_FROM` | `bok-scribe-seal-openclaw-v1` | `bok-scribe-seal-gamificacao-nodes-v1` | suite lineage (latest BoK seals) |\n| `observes` (OE-661-003 pattern) | observation node | ast_file / markdown_file | when openclaw audit references specific file paths |\n| `DEFINES` | `pantheon-role-matrix.md` markdown_file | agent observation nodes | semantic anchoring |\n\n### Embedding Trigger\n\nNenhum dado runtime do OpenClaw dispara embedding diretamente \u2014 toda telemetria flui via `infra_health_logs` (n\u00e3o embedded por constru\u00e7\u00e3o, reten\u00e7\u00e3o 90d) ou via observation nodes manuais (embedded por trigger `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` pg_net 768d).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Tenant Isolation Map\n\n| Resource | Scope | Notes |\n|----------|-------|-------|\n| `~/.openclaw/` filesystem | Single Sovereign | chmod 700 enforced; sem multi-user model |\n| Gateway `:18789` | Loopback only | bind=loopback; trustedProxies=[127.0.0.1, ::1] |\n| `infra_health_logs` (Supabase) | Service role write; authenticated read | RLS permite SELECT a authenticated; INSERT s\u00f3 via service role |\n| `mcorch_nodes` observation nodes para OpenClaw | `user_id IS NULL` (system) | Vis\u00edveis a todos tenants (audits e seals) |\n| Memory SQLite per-agente | Filesystem-isolated por agent dir | Sem boundary cross-agent intencional (agentes operam em namespace pr\u00f3prio) |\n| Memory Wiki vault | Single Sovereign | `~/.openclaw/wiki/main/` chmod 755 |\n\n**Invariante:** OpenClaw \u00e9 single-Sovereign trust boundary. Multi-user heuristic do audit-v1 (I.1) \u00e9 informativa, n\u00e3o bloqueante \u2014 registrado em OTD-CM-006 e aceito (`A-001` em \u00a77 de 02-brd).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Quality Rules\n\n| Resource | Field | Rule | Violation Message (PT-BR) |\n|----------|-------|------|--------------------------|\n| `openclaw.json` | `agents.list[].id` | \u00danico; sem duplicatas | \"ID de agente duplicado \u2014 squad inconsistente\" |\n| `openclaw.json` | `gateway.auth.token` | \u2265 32 chars, hex random | \"Token de gateway insuficiente \u2014 gere com `openssl rand -hex 24`\" |\n| `openclaw.json` | `gateway.controlUi.allowInsecureAuth` | strictly `false` | \"Modo inseguro detectado \u2014 Control UI exposta sem device check\" |\n| `openclaw.json` | `agents.list[].enabled` | **proibido** (schema strict v2026.5.14) | \"Campo `enabled` rejeitado \u2014 remover entry; gate \u00e9 via `agents.defaults.subagents.allowAgents[]`\" |\n| `secrets.json` | filesystem perms | chmod 600 | \"Secrets vault leg\u00edvel por outros \u2014 exfiltra\u00e7\u00e3o poss\u00edvel\" |\n| `~/.openclaw/` | filesystem perms | chmod 700 | \"Diret\u00f3rio OpenClaw leg\u00edvel por outros \u2014 leak de configs e mem\u00f3ria\" |\n| Memory Wiki frontmatter | `pageType` | enum v\u00e1lido | \"pageType desconhecido \u2014 Memory Palace n\u00e3o renderizar\u00e1 a p\u00e1gina\" |\n| Memory Wiki frontmatter | `id` | URL-safe slug, \u00fanico per pageType | \"ID inv\u00e1lido \u2014 wiki linter rejeitar\u00e1 na pr\u00f3xima compile\" |\n| `manifest.json` (mesh export) | `bridge_version` | semver | \"Bridge version drift detectado \u2014 re-rodar `bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts`\" |\n| SQLite per-agente | `documents.path` | path relativo a agent memory dir | \"Path absoluto detectado \u2014 re-indexar com `--agent=<id>` para normalizar\" |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Retention & Archival Policy\n\n| Data | Retention Period | Archival Strategy | Deletion Trigger |\n|------|-----------------|-------------------|-----------------|\n| `openclaw.json` | Indefinido | Git-tracked n\u00e3o \u2014 mas backups manuais antes de jq surgical | Sovereign decision (rare) |\n| `~/.openclaw/secrets.json` | Indefinido enquanto chaves v\u00e1lidas | Sem archival; rota\u00e7\u00e3o substitui | Rota\u00e7\u00e3o de chave individual |\n| Memory SQLite per-agente | Indefinido | Backup di\u00e1rio via Stage 1 spot check (TODO future) | Quando agente \u00e9 removido do squad (uninstall flow) |\n| Mesh export (`memory/mcorch-export/`) | Refresh di\u00e1rio (Stage 2) | Sem archival; arquivo \u00e9 recomput\u00e1vel idempotentemente | Refresh sobrescreve |\n| Memory Wiki pages | Indefinido | Backup mensal | `delete` via Control UI ou linter rejection ap\u00f3s drift |\n| `infra_health_logs` (openclaw-*) | 90 dias | Cron purge | Auto via retention policy global |\n| Watchdog incident JSONs (`/tmp/openclaw-incidents/`) | 30 dias | Sem archival | Cron `/tmp` cleanup ou Stage 1 |\n| Dreaming logs (`~/.openclaw/agents/<id>/memory/dreaming/`) | 14 dias | Promoted recalls v\u00e3o para MEMORY.md long-term | Cron 05:00 (OpenClaw native) |\n\n**LGPD compliance:** OpenClaw lida APENAS com dados do Sovereign (single-user). Sem PII de terceiros em produ\u00e7\u00e3o. `data-export` Edge Function do MCORCH n\u00e3o inclui artefatos OpenClaw (fora do escopo LGPD do MCORCH user-facing).\n\n---\n\n## 8. Data Flow Diagrams\n\n### 3.1 Bridge Refresh (Stage 2 \u2014 05:00 BRT)\n\n```\n[Cron 05:00 BRT] \u2500\u2192 [nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh]\n                         \u2502\n                         \u251c\u2500\u2192 [nightly-bridge-refresh.sh]\n                         \u2502      \u2502\n                         \u2502      \u251c\u2500\u2192 [bridge-mesh-to-openclaw.ts]\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 SELECT * FROM mcorch_nodes (service role)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 Filter exclude `ast_variable` (5336 trivial entries)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 Split tier1 strategy (375) + tier2 AST (2527)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 Write memory/mcorch-export/{tier1,tier2,_meta}/\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 Generate edges.jsonl + manifest.json\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2514\u2500\u2192 exit 0 (idempotente)\n                         \u2502      \u2502\n                         \u2502      \u251c\u2500\u2192 [distribute-mesh-to-agents.ts]\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 For each agent \u2208 [engineer, scientist, artisan, marketing-growth, guardian, claw-master]:\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   - Copy shared base (decision, milestone, system, handoff)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   - Apply overlay filter por lente BoK\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   - File copy (n\u00e3o symlink \u2014 indexer n\u00e3o segue)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2514\u2500\u2192 exit 0\n                         \u2502      \u2502\n                         \u2502      \u2514\u2500\u2192 [openclaw memory index --agent=<id>] \u00d7 6 agents\n                         \u2502             (OTD-V660-002: subcomando drift; workaround via REST direto)\n                         \u2502\n                         \u251c\u2500\u2192 Ledger drift watch alert-only (queries mcoin_transactions)\n                         \u251c\u2500\u2192 VACUUM ANALYZE quando pooler dispon\u00edvel\n                         \u2514\u2500\u2192 INSERT infra_health_logs(service='openclaw-stage2', status='healthy')\n```\n\n### 3.2 Morning Briefing (Stage 3 \u2014 07:00 BRT)\n\n```\n[Cron 07:00 BRT] \u2500\u2192 [nightly-stage3-rem.sh]\n                         \u2502\n                         \u251c\u2500\u2192 [news-impact-analyzer.ts]\n                         \u2502      \u2502 OpenRouter perplexity/sonar request\n                         \u2502      \u2502 5 lentes web-grounded (revenue/tech/regulatory/competition/Paz e Vitalidade)\n                         \u2502      \u2502 Output: news_pulse observation node em mcorch_nodes\n                         \u2502      \u2514\u2500\u2192 embed-mcorch-node trigger autoembed\n                         \u2502\n                         \u251c\u2500\u2192 [morning-briefing.sh]\n                         \u2502      \u2502\n                         \u2502      \u251c\u2500\u2192 Load Telegram botToken from vault (jq direct read secrets.json:/keys/telegramBotToken)\n                         \u2502      \u2502\n                         \u2502      \u251c\u2500\u2192 [compose-morning-briefing.ts]\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 6 se\u00e7\u00f5es markdown:\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   (1) Git delta \u00faltimas 24h\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   (2) MEMORY.md promotions per agent\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   (3) BoK debt registry\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   (4) Revenue snippet (gamificacao ledger drift watch)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   (5) News pulse (read latest news_pulse node)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502   (6) Paz e Vitalidade reflex\u00e3o (5\u00aa lente OE03)\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2502 Truncate to \u2264 4000 chars\n                         \u2502      \u2502      \u2514\u2500\u2192 stdout markdown\n                         \u2502      \u2502\n                         \u2502      \u2514\u2500\u2192 POST https://api.telegram.org/bot$TOKEN/sendMessage\n                         \u2502             body: { chat_id: 5835174772, text: <markdown>, parse_mode: 'Markdown' }\n                         \u2502             on success: msg_id incrementa; log INSERT infra_health_logs(service='openclaw-briefing', status='delivered')\n                         \u2502             on quota error: log + retry no pr\u00f3ximo Stage 3 (idempotente \u2014 n\u00e3o h\u00e1 refund/state)\n                         \u2502\n                         \u2514\u2500\u2192 exit 0\n```\n\n### 3.3 Watchdog Layer 2 Escalation\n\n```\n[Cron */5 * * * *] \u2500\u2192 [watchdog-mcorch.sh]\n                            \u2502\n                            \u251c\u2500\u2192 docker ps mcorch_chroma \u00b7 mcorch_claude_mem \u00b7 mega-brain-daemon\n                            \u251c\u2500\u2192 curl http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat (Chroma API v2)\n                            \u2502\n                            \u251c\u2500 healthy \u2500\u2192 Reset counter `/tmp/mcorch-watchdog-fail-counter` to 0\n                            \u2502             INSERT infra_health_logs(service='watchdog', status='healthy')\n                            \u2502\n                            \u2514\u2500 unhealthy \u2500\u2192 counter += 1\n                                            \u2502\n                                            \u251c\u2500 counter < 3 \u2500\u2192 log retry; exit\n                                            \u2502\n                                            \u2514\u2500 counter \u2265 3 \u2500\u2192 [Layer 2 escalation]\n                                                                \u2502\n                                                                \u251c\u2500\u2192 Write /tmp/openclaw-incidents/<timestamp>-<service>.json\n                                                                \u2502   { service, counter, last_failure, observed_at }\n                                                                \u2502\n                                                                \u251c\u2500\u2192 POST best-effort gateway 127.0.0.1:18789/incident\n                                                                \u2502   (authorization: bearer token)\n                                                                \u2502\n                                                                \u251c\u2500\u2192 INSERT infra_health_logs(service='openclaw-escalation', status='incident')\n                                                                \u2502\n                                                                \u2514\u2500 Layer 1 (auto-restart) fallback: docker compose restart <service>\n                                                                   Counter resets only on next healthy pulse (idempotent)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. API Contracts\n\n### 4.1 OpenClaw CLI subcommands relevantes\n\n| Subcommand | Purpose | Failure modes |\n|------------|---------|---------------|\n| `openclaw config validate` | Valida `openclaw.json` schema strict | \"Unrecognized key\" \u2192 drift D-OC-004 pattern |\n| `openclaw config get <path>` | L\u00ea config (consumido pela UI) | Retorna `null` se schema inv\u00e1lido |\n| `openclaw config set <path> <value>` | Edita config | Backup autom\u00e1tico em /tmp |\n| `openclaw daemon start/stop/restart` | Gerencia gateway daemon | PID em /var/run/openclaw |\n| `openclaw daemon status` | Liveness check | Retorna `{\"ok\":true, \"status\":\"live\"}` quando healthy |\n| `openclaw memory index --agent=<id>` | Re-indexa SQLite per-agente | **Drift** OTD-V660-002 \u2014 subcomando n\u00e3o funciona; workaround: bridge script + REST |\n| `openclaw security audit [--fix]` | Audit secrets + perms + binding | Plain text scan + perms check; `--fix` autocorrige perms |\n| `openclaw wiki ingest <file>` | Manual ingest de wiki page | Frontmatter validation; lint pr\u00e9-apply |\n| `openclaw plugin list` | Lista plugins enabled/disabled | \"72/122 enabled\" pattern atual |\n\n### 4.2 Gateway HTTP endpoints (loopback only)\n\n```\nGET  /health                        \u2192 200 {\"status\":\"live\"}             (no auth needed)\nGET  /heartbeat                     \u2192 200 {\"timestamp\":<iso>, ...}      (token required)\nPOST /incident                      \u2192 202 (best-effort from watchdog)   (token required)\nGET  /control-ui/config             \u2192 200 <openclaw.json subset>        (device auth + token)\nPOST /control-ui/plugins/toggle     \u2192 200/403                            (device auth + token)\n```\n\n### 4.3 Telegram POST contract (briefing delivery)\n\n```\nPOST https://api.telegram.org/bot<BOT_TOKEN>/sendMessage\nContent-Type: application/json\n\n{\n  \"chat_id\": 5835174772,\n  \"text\": \"<markdown briefing \u2264 4000 chars>\",\n  \"parse_mode\": \"Markdown\"\n}\n\nResponse 200 OK:\n{ \"ok\": true, \"result\": { \"message_id\": <incremental int>, \"chat\": {...}, \"date\": <unix> } }\n\nErrors:\n  400 \u2014 markdown parse error \u2192 retry plain text fallback (TODO)\n  429 \u2014 rate limit (free tier 30 msg/s) \u2192 retry com backoff\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Security Design (CISSP CBK + STRIDE)\n\n### 5.1 Trust Boundaries\n\n```\n+--------------------------------------------------------------+\n| OUTER BOUNDARY (untrusted internet)                          |\n|  - Cloudflare DNS                                            |\n|  - claw.gcrux.com (Cloudflare protected; cert pinning OK)    |\n+----+---------------------------------------------------------+\n     \u2502 HTTPS only (TLS 1.3)\n     \u25bc\n+--------------------------------------------------------------+\n| MIDDLE BOUNDARY (server perimeter \u2014 nginx/cloudflared)       |\n|  - nginx termina TLS                                         |\n|  - Proxy a 127.0.0.1:18789 (loopback)                       |\n|  - trustedProxies: [127.0.0.1, ::1]                         |\n+----+---------------------------------------------------------+\n     \u2502 HTTP loopback only (no external exposure)\n     \u25bc\n+--------------------------------------------------------------+\n| INNER BOUNDARY (gateway daemon)                              |\n|  - auth.mode: 'token'                                        |\n|  - token >= 32 chars hex                                     |\n|  - controlUi.allowInsecureAuth: false                       |\n|  - controlUi.allowedOrigins explicit                         |\n+----+---------------------------------------------------------+\n     \u2502 Token validation\n     \u25bc\n+--------------------------------------------------------------+\n| SOVEREIGN ENCLAVE (single trust principal)                   |\n|  - filesystem chmod 700 (group/other denied)                 |\n|  - secrets.json chmod 600                                    |\n|  - openclaw.json chmod 600                                   |\n|  - SecretRef pointers (zero plain-text)                     |\n+--------------------------------------------------------------+\n```\n\n### 5.2 STRIDE Threat Model\n\n| Threat | Surface | Mitigation | Residual Risk |\n|--------|---------|------------|---------------|\n| **S**poofing \u2014 token leak permite spoof | Gateway HTTP | Loopback bind + 48-char hex token + rotation manual | Low (token nunca sai do server) |\n| **T**ampering \u2014 config edit acidental quebra schema | openclaw.json | Backup autom\u00e1tico antes de jq surgical; `config validate` antes de daemon restart | Low |\n| **R**epudiation \u2014 quem alterou config? | openclaw.json | meta.lastTouchedAt + meta.lastTouchedVersion; git hist\u00f3rico parcial via backups | Medium (n\u00e3o h\u00e1 audit trail strong; aceito Y1) |\n| **I**nformation Disclosure \u2014 secrets em config | openclaw.json | Vault file-based SecretRef (reconciliado v6.4.0); zero plain-text | Low |\n| **D**enial of Service \u2014 daemon crash silencia squad | Gateway | Watchdog Layer 2 detecta e escalon a; Sovereign alertado no briefing | Medium (briefing pode atrasar diagnose) |\n| **E**levation of Privilege \u2014 agente sai do Decision Scope | Squad runtime | SOUL.md como contrato; main consulta routing-map; guardian sinaliza self-optimization | Medium (escope drift \u00e9 detection-only Y1) |\n\n### 5.3 Secrets Lifecycle\n\n```\nGenerate \u2500\u2500\u2192 Sovereign creates new key/token via vendor portal\n       \u2502\n       \u25bc\nStore \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2192 INSERT into ~/.openclaw/secrets.json:/keys/<name>\n       \u2502   (single file, chmod 600, owner ubuntu)\n       \u2502\n       \u25bc\nReference \u2500\u2192 openclaw.json points via SecretRef:\n              { \"source\": \"file\", \"provider\": \"filemain\", \"id\": \"/keys/<name>\" }\n       \u2502\n       \u25bc\nConsume \u2500\u2500\u2192 At runtime, OpenClaw plugin (e.g. telegram) reads from filemain provider\n       \u2502   (transparent to plugin code)\n       \u2502\n       \u25bc\nRotate \u2500\u2500\u2192 Manual: Sovereign updates secrets.json entry; daemon restart picks up\n       \u2502   (no automatic rotation Y1)\n       \u2502\n       \u25bc\nRevoke \u2500\u2500\u2192 Delete entry from secrets.json + vendor portal revoke + daemon restart\n```\n\n### 5.4 Default Deny Posture\n\n| Surface | Default | Rationale |\n|---------|---------|-----------|\n| Filesystem perms | `700` (chmod) | Sovereign-only; group/other denied |\n| Gateway binding | `loopback` | Sem exposi\u00e7\u00e3o externa direta |\n| Tailscale | `off` | N\u00e3o h\u00e1 squad humano remoto Y1 |\n| Plugins | Boolean gate per plugin (`enabled: true/false`) | Default Y1: ~50 plugins disabled de 122 total |\n| Node commands | `denyCommands[]` lista alta-impact (camera/screen/sms) | Default deny para comandos de OS sens\u00edveis |\n| Memory Wiki linter | Strict frontmatter validation | P\u00e1ginas malformadas rejeitadas (n\u00e3o silencioso) |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| External system | Direction | Coupling | Notes |\n|-----------------|-----------|----------|-------|\n| Supabase `mcorch_nodes` | OpenClaw reads | Tight (bridge depends on schema) | Read-only via service role; schema changes upstream invalidam bridge |\n| Supabase `infra_health_logs` | OpenClaw writes | Loose (best-effort, fail-open) | Insert via service role; nunca bloqueia operation |\n| Telegram Bot API | OpenClaw writes | Loose (best-effort) | Free tier; quota gentle; retry no pr\u00f3ximo Stage 3 |\n| OpenRouter `perplexity/sonar` | OpenClaw reads (Stage 3) | Loose (skip se vendor down) | Web-grounded; sem cache local Y1 (OTD-V660-004 monitora low signal) |\n| Firecrawl WebSearch | claw-master reads (mensal) | Loose | Scrape `docs.openclaw.ai`; idempotente; refresh marker em metadata |\n| `mcorch_chroma` (Docker) | Watchdog probes | Loose | Liveness check apenas |\n| `mega-brain-daemon` (Docker) | Watchdog probes + escalation publisher | Medium | Gateway de Layer 2 incidents para infra_health_logs |\n| `mcorch_claude_mem` (Docker) | Bridge consumer indirect | Loose | ARM64 substitute, n\u00e3o-cr\u00edtico para OpenClaw |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Migration Stubs\n\nOpenClaw **n\u00e3o introduz migrations Supabase**. Toda persist\u00eancia runtime \u00e9 local (filesystem + SQLite per-agente). Apenas writes em `infra_health_logs` (tabela existente).\n\n**Schema-relevant changes** ao longo da vida do m\u00f3dulo:\n- v6.3.1: 7 agentes registrados em `agents.list[]` (Pantheon escalation)\n- v6.4.0: 6 SecretRefs em `secrets.providers.filemain` (vault migration); `gateway.controlUi.allowInsecureAuth = false`; `chmod 700 ~/.openclaw`\n- v6.6.1: jq surgical `del(.enabled)` em `agents.list[]` entries (schema strict drift); 20 wiki pages seed manual\n\n**Future expected:**\n- Add `gateway.auth.rateLimit` block (OTD-CM-004)\n- Add `logging.redactSensitive: true` (OTD-CM-005)\n- Add `marketing-growth/BOOTSTRAP.md` scaffold (D-OC-008)\n\n---\n\n## 8. monitor crescimento; B se > 1 GB | Decided 2026-05-19 (revisita Q3 2026) |\n| OTD-OC-002 | SQLite memory schema \u00e9 opaco upstream (`docs.openclaw.ai` n\u00e3o publica) | A: Reverse-engineer via dump pragma \u00b7 B: Tratar como blackbox \u00b7 C: Abrir issue upstream | C \u2014 abrir issue upstream pedindo schema doc | Decided 2026-05-19 |\n| OTD-OC-003 | Multi-user expansion \u2014 quando squad ganhar segundo humano (cliente externo) | A: Adicionar rate-limit + per-user namespace \u00b7 B: Spin nova inst\u00e2ncia OpenClaw isolada \u00b7 C: Bloquear sob Sovereign-only Y1 | C Y1; A quando Sprint Commercial \u2265 5 clientes | Decided 2026-05-19 |\n| OTD-OC-004 | Briefing fallback quando Telegram quota exhausted | A: Buffer markdown em arquivo local + retry \u00b7 B: Email fallback \u00b7 C: Aceitar miss e re-trying pr\u00f3ximo dia | A Y1 (arquivo local em /var/log/mcorch-briefing-fallback/) | Decided 2026-05-19 |\n| OTD-OC-005 | Dynamic plugin loading (sem daemon restart) | A: Aguardar upstream feature \u00b7 B: Implementar wrapper signal handler \u00b7 C: Aceitar restart por edit | A \u2014 aguardar upstream | Decided 2026-05-19 |\n| **OTD-V660-002** (herdada) | OpenClaw CLI `openclaw memory index` subcomando n\u00e3o existe v2026.5.14 | A: Bridge script substitui \u00b7 B: Aguardar upstream | A | Decided 2026-05-16 |\n| **OTD-CM-004** (herdada audit-v1) | `gateway.auth.rateLimit` ausente | A: Adicionar bloco \u00b7 B: Aceitar single-Sovereign | A quando squad ganhar 2\u00ba humano; Non-Functional Requirements\n\n| ID | Requirement | Target | Verification |\n|----|-------------|--------|--------------|\n| NFR-OC-1 | Performance: Stage 2 bridge refresh p95 \u2264 180s | end-to-end (export + distribute + index 6 agentes) | Timer `time scripts/nightly-stage2-deep-sleep.sh` \u2265 28/30 dias dentro de threshold |\n| NFR-OC-2 | Security: filesystem perms `~/.openclaw` = 700; `secrets.json` = 600; `openclaw.json` = 600 | stat -c \"%a\" | Audit mensal pelo claw-master |\n| NFR-OC-3 | Reliability: Briefing delivery rate \u2265 95% / 30 dias | `service='openclaw-briefing' AND status='delivered'` count \u2265 28 | Monthly query infra_health_logs |\n| NFR-OC-4 | Scalability: SQLite per-agente growth \u2264 20 MB/dia | du -sm ~/.openclaw/memory/*.sqlite | Stage 1 spot check; alert se > 20 |\n| NFR-OC-5 | Maintainability: Scripts pipeline (bridge, distribute, briefing) idempotentes \u2014 re-run produz mesmo resultado | Hash diff entre runs consecutivos = 0 (excluding timestamps) | CI manual quando script edita |\n| NFR-OC-6 | Observability: Stage 1/2/3 + watchdog + escalation \u2192 INSERT infra_health_logs sempre | service='openclaw-*' rows \u2265 3/dia esperado (1 por stage) | Daily count query |\n| NFR-OC-7 | Security: Zero plain-text secrets em config | grep regex retorna 0 matches | claw-master audit mensal |\n| NFR-OC-8 | Disaster Recovery: Backup `openclaw.json` antes de qualquer edit destrutivo (jq surgical) | /tmp/openclaw.backup-*.json timestamped | Script hooks j\u00e1 enforced |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Interface Requirements\n\n### 4.1 OpenClaw CLI Interface\n\n| Caller | Subcommand | Args | Response shape |\n|--------|-----------|------|---------------|\n| Scripts MCORCH (Stage 2) | `node ~/openclaw/openclaw.mjs config validate` | \u2014 | exit 0 + \"Config valid\" stdout |\n| Scripts MCORCH (Stage 1) | `node ~/openclaw/openclaw.mjs daemon status` | \u2014 | JSON `{\"ok\":true,\"status\":\"live\"}` |\n| Sovereign (manual) | `node ~/openclaw/openclaw.mjs security audit` | `[--fix]` | Findings list + RPN scores |\n| claw-master (manual) | `node ~/openclaw/openclaw.mjs plugin list` | \u2014 | `Plugins (72/122 enabled)` + listagem |\n| Stage 3 briefing | (bash direct, sem CLI) | \u2014 | \u2014 |\n\n### 4.2 HTTP Interface (Gateway)\n\n| Caller | Endpoint | Auth | Response |\n|--------|----------|------|---------|\n| Watchdog | `GET /heartbeat` | Bearer token | 200 + JSON liveness |\n| Watchdog Layer 2 | `POST /incident` | Bearer token | 202 (best-effort) |\n| Control UI browser | `GET /control-ui/*` | Device auth + token | Web UI assets + JSON config |\n| Memory Wiki UI | `GET /wiki/main/*` | Device auth + token | Markdown rendered |\n\n### 4.3 External APIs\n\n| Direction | Service | Endpoint | Auth |\n|-----------|---------|----------|------|\n| Egress | Telegram Bot API | `https://api.telegram.org/bot$TOKEN/sendMessage` | botToken via vault |\n| Egress | OpenRouter | `https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions` | OPENROUTER_API_KEY env |\n| Egress | Firecrawl | `https://api.firecrawl.dev/v1/search` | apiKey via vault |\n| Egress | Supabase REST | `https://<project>.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes` | SERVICE_ROLE_KEY |\n| Egress | Supabase REST | `https://<project>.supabase.co/rest/v1/infra_health_logs` | SERVICE_ROLE_KEY |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Requirements\n\n- **Persistence:** Local filesystem (`~/.openclaw/`) + SQLite per-agente + Supabase `infra_health_logs` + `mcorch_nodes` (observation/handoff/documentation_suite tipos)\n- **Sensitive data:** Secrets em vault file-based (chmod 600); zero plain-text em `openclaw.json`\n- **Data movement:** mcorch_nodes (read) \u2192 memory/mcorch-export (filesystem) \u2192 ~/.openclaw/agents/<id>/memory (file copies)\n- **Audit trail:** `infra_health_logs` 90-day retention; backups manuais antes de jq surgical em `openclaw.json`\n- **PII:** Nenhum PII de terceiros (single-Sovereign trust boundary); apenas dados operacionais\n\n---\n\n## 6. com token \u2192 200\n- [ ] **FR-OC-6:** Memory Palace UI renderiza \u2265 5 syntheses + Imported Insights renderiza \u2265 1 cluster\n- [ ] **FR-OC-7:** Stage 3 dry-run POSTs Telegram com 200 OK + msg_id retornado\n- [ ] **NFR-OC-1:** Stage 2 timing p95 \u2264 180s sustained \u2265 28/30 dias (KPI-OC-001)\n- [ ] **NFR-OC-2:** `stat -c \"%a\" ~/.openclaw` = 700; Problema\n\nSovereign \u00fanico enfrenta **teto cognitivo** quando precisa alternar entre lentes BoK distintas (engineering \u00b7 science \u00b7 UX \u00b7 growth \u00b7 workflow \u00b7 security). For\u00e7ar uma \u00fanica inst\u00e2ncia de IA a operar com m\u00faltiplas lentes degrada qualidade em cada uma. Sem **mem\u00f3ria persistente cross-session**, cada conversa come\u00e7a do zero \u2014 o que rec\u00e9m foi decidido se perde. Sem **awareness loop di\u00e1rio**, Sovereign perde rastro do que mudou enquanto n\u00e3o estava olhando.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Solu\u00e7\u00e3o\n\nSistema de **Pantheon Architecture** que materializa 7 agentes IA especializados como processos isolados em runtime local, cada um com:\n- SOUL.md como contrato de identidade (Purpose \u00b7 BoK Lens \u00b7 Decision Scope \u2705/\u274c \u00b7 Tools)\n- Mem\u00f3ria SQLite persistente com FTS5 + vector embeddings\n- Overlay BoK-espec\u00edfica da Knowledge Mesh MCORCH (n\u00e3o c\u00f3pia bruta)\n- Ciclo de Memory Dreaming Promotion (recalls \u2192 MEMORY.md long-term)\n\nMais um **Telegram briefing di\u00e1rio** entregue \u00e0s 07:30 BRT pelo guardian agent, sintetizando git delta + MEMORY.md promotions + BoK debt + revenue snippet + news pulse + reflex\u00e3o Paz e Vitalidade.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Proposta de Valor\n\n| Stakeholder | Benef\u00edcio |\n|-------------|-----------|\n| Sovereign (Gabriel) | Pantheon de IAs especializadas sem custo cognitivo proporcional; briefing di\u00e1rio autom\u00e1tico; soberania f\u00edsica (gateway loopback) |\n| `main` agent | Roteia decis\u00e3o certa para o Head certo via routing-map autoritativo |\n| L1 Heads (engineer, scientist, artisan, marketing-growth) | Operam dentro de Decision Scope claro com overlay BoK-espec\u00edfica |\n| `guardian` | Comp\u00f5e briefing + observa dream cycle + prop\u00f5e self-optimization PRs |\n| `claw-master` (L2) | Audit OpenClaw vs upstream mensalmente sem executar fixes |\n| MCORCH como produto | Velocity 6\u00d7 sobre baseline single-agent; qualidade BoK enforced |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Causa raiz: `canvas-execute` retorna HTTP 200 + `error` field, `mutateAsync` não throw, frontend tratava qualquer 200 como sucesso. Novo util `src/lib/canvas-execute-result.ts` faz `assertExecuteSuccess<T>(result)` que THROW em `result.error` set OU `status ∈ {failed,timeout,cancelled}`. Aplicado em 4 inspectors + Run-All. Toast agora usa `result.credits_charged ?? cost` (zero quando falha). Migration `20260518012541_canvas_assets_update_policy.sql` adiciona UPDATE policy (necessária para `upload({upsert:true})` que vira `INSERT...ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`). **(Round 3 — 4 bugs novos)** **(A)** OpenAI `dall-e-3` E `gpt-image-1` HTTP 400 `Unknown parameter: 'response_format'`. OTD-CV42C-001 registrada para Phase 4.3 (picker dinâmico via OR `/v1/models` com free tier). **(C)** Replicate "key not configured" — coluna `replicate_api_key` NUNCA existia em `user_api_keys`. Migration `20260518020028_user_api_keys_replicate.sql` adiciona. `types.ts` ganha campo manual em Row/Insert/Update (evita full regen). `SettingsPage` ganha KeyField. Inspector ganha banner per-provider (openai/openrouter/replicate) quando user key falta com link `/dashboard/settings`. **(D)** Storage RLS 403 persistente em character upload. Investigação revelou NOTICE crítico no `db push`: `policy "Public read canvas assets" does not exist, skipping` — confirma que migration original `20260514040200` aplicou apenas 3 de 4 policies no remoto. Migration `20260518020030_canvas_assets_rls_repair.sql` (idempotente, DROP IF EXISTS 8 nomes + recria 4 policies) usa `TO public + auth.uid() IS NOT NULL` (defensivo contra publishable key `sb_publishable_*` que pode não resolver `TO authenticated`) + `owner = auth.uid()` fallback em UPDATE/DELETE. **Material proofs:** TS zero erros · 122/122 testes em 4.40s · build em 23.55s · `CanvasEditorPage` chunk 113.66 kB · `canvas-execute` 138.3 kB · 4 commits granulares (`6554cb4` canvas src · `76c99e3` 3 migrations · `9615c69` readme · `0d7bc07` handoff) · push `cc828a0..0d7bc07` para origin/main · milestone node `eb0b3ae0-9ec1-4328-ad31-1b079d3f294e` embedado (768 dims) · saldo Sovereign **5533** intacto · mesh total **8336 nodes** (+8 desde v6.6.1). **Próximo deliverable:** (a) smoke test pago DoP Standard 5s ($0.56) — Sovereign valida gates 4-9 da SOP; (b) cron install do watchdog (`crontab -e` adicionando `*/5 * * * * scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh`) após smoke passar; (c) OTD-CV42C-001 dynamic OR model picker — Phase 4.3 spec dedicada.

## PHASE 1 — SECURITY AUDIT

Run these checks and report findings. **Block the seal if any CRITICAL finding exists.**

> ⚠️ **SCOPE NOTE:** This phase scans changes that exist NOW (before Phase 5 writes HANDOFF.md).
> A second mandatory scan runs in Phase 5b, after HANDOFF.md is written and before it is committed.

```bash
# 1a. Secret leak scan — check staged + working tree for hardcoded credentials
git diff HEAD | grep -E "(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{20,}|STRIPE_|SECRET_KEY|ACCESS_TOKEN|api_key\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,})" \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_" | head -20

# 1b. Edge function JWT enforcement — every user-facing function must verify JWT
grep -rL "Authorization\|jwt\|JWT\|service_role" supabase/functions/*/index.ts \
  | grep -v "get-infra-status\|watchdog" || echo "ALL FUNCTIONS: JWT enforced ✅"

# 1c. Client-side coin deduction guard — must NEVER update mco_balance directly from client
grep -rn "mco_balance.*update\|UPDATE.*mco_balance" src/ \
  | grep -v "settings\|top.up\|topup\|SettingsPage" | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1d. RLS bypass risk — check for supabase.rpc calls without auth context
grep -rn "service_role\|bypass.*rls\|rls.*bypass" src/ | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1e. Sensitive data in console.log
grep -rn "console\.log.*token\|console\.log.*key\|console\.log.*secret\|console\.log.*password" src/ \
  | head -10 || echo "NO SENSITIVE LOGS ✅"
```

**Security verdict:** list each check as ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / 🔴 BLOCK.
Only proceed if no 🔴 findings.

### DOCUMENTATION CREDENTIAL RULE (invariant — never violate)

**When writing HANDOFF.md, README.md, or any committed documentation:**

- ✅ DO: describe WHERE a credential is stored → `VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY` is set in `.env` (local) and `GEMINI_API_KEY` in the Supabase vault
- ✅ DO: reference digest/fingerprint if useful → `GEMINI_API_KEY (digest 0fe0e159...)`
- 🔴 NEVER: include the actual credential value → `AIzaSyBv...`, `sk-...`, `eyJ...`

> **Rationale:** Phase 1 scans changes that predate the HANDOFF.md write. Any credential embedded
> in HANDOFF.md during Phase 5 escapes Phase 1 entirely and goes directly to the remote.
> This rule is the primary prevention layer; Phase 5b is the detection backstop.

```bash
source .env
SUPABASE_URL="https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co"
SESSION_PHASE="<phase-name-slug>"
SESSION_SUMMARY="<one-sentence summary of what was accomplished>"

curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes" \
  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Prefer: return=representation" \
  -d "{
    \"name\": \"session-handoff-$(date +%Y%m%d)-${SESSION_PHASE}\",
    \"node_type\": \"handoff\",
    \"content\": \"[HANDOFF SEAL] ${SESSION_SUMMARY}\",
    \"stability_score\": 1.0,
    \"project_id\": null,
    \"user_id\": null,
    \"metadata\": {

      \"commits\": \"$(git log --oneline -5 | head -5 | tr '\n' '|')\"

  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \

  || echo "⚠️ Embedding failed — node persisted but not vectorized"
```

  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_\|digest\|sha256\|hash\|fingerprint" \
  | head -20 || echo "HANDOFF.md: NO CREDENTIALS FOUND ✅"

# 5b-2. Specifically check for Google API keys (AIza prefix = 39 chars total)

## PHASE 6 — BoK SEAL STATUS (mandatory before push)

**Verify any significant module work in this session has a sealed BoK suite per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.**

```bash
# 6-1. List BoK suites present + check completeness
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] \
    && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete (9 docs)" \
    || echo "⚠️ $slug — incomplete (missing: ${missing[*]})"
done

# 6-2. Check session touched code in new src/pages/ or new supabase/functions/ that lacks BoK
session_new_modules=$(git diff --name-only HEAD~10..HEAD 2>/dev/null | grep -E "^(src/pages/|supabase/functions/|src/components/[A-Z])" | awk -F/ '{print "/"}' | sort -u)
echo "Session touched modules: $session_new_modules"

# 6-3. Mesh seal nodes for sealed BoK suites
source .env 2>/dev/null
if [ -n "$SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" ]; then
  curl -s "$SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?node_type=eq.documentation_suite&select=id,name,stability_score,created_at" \
    -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); [print(f\"  🌐 {n['name']} ({n['stability_score']})\") for n in d]" 2>/dev/null \
    || echo "  ⚠️ Could not query seal nodes"
fi
```

**Gate verdict:**
- ✅ All session modules have sealed BoK → proceed to Phase 7 push.
- ⚠️ Module work without BoK (e.g. `feat:` commit creating new page/edge fn without `docs/bok/<slug>/`)
  → 🔴 **BLOCK seal**. Run `/bok-scribe <module>` first OR add module to exemption list (typo/dep bump/single-file patch).

Report BoK status in final seal output (Phase 8).

---

## PHASE 7 — PUSH

```bash
git push origin main
```

Report: branch, number of commits pushed, remote URL.

If push fails (non-fast-forward): run `git pull --rebase origin main` first, then push again. Never force-push main.

---

## PHASE 7b — SPRINT & CONNECTIONS HYGIENE

### Sprint priorities update

Review `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` and update:
1. Check off completed items in "Skills a criar ou evoluir" and "Conexões a implementar"
2. Add new gaps discovered this session under "Top 3 Gap Closures" if applicable
3. Update the 4Cs Audit Snapshot scores if `/audit` was run this session
4. Append to the Retrospective section if this was the last session of the sprint

Commit if changed:
```
chore(sprint): update sprint-priorities — <one-line progress note>
```

### Scratch cleanup

```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null
```

If `scratch/` has `.ts` diagnostic scripts, move them to `.claude/scripts/db/`:
```bash
mkdir -p .claude/scripts/db
mv scratch/*.ts .claude/scripts/db/ 2>/dev/null && echo "Moved ✅" || echo "Nothing to move"
```

Commit if files were moved:
```
chore(scripts): migrate scratch diagnostics to .claude/scripts/db/
```

### MCP documentation check

```bash
cat .mcp.json
```

For each MCP server listed, verify it has an entry in `.claude/references/` or is documented in CLAUDE.md. If a new MCP was added this session and has no documentation, add a one-liner to the relevant reference file.

---

## PHASE 8 — FINAL REPORT

Print seal summary in **Portuguese (Brasil)**:

```
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  HANDOFF SEALED — <PHASE NAME> (<date>)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

🔐 SEGURANÇA
  <security check results — each as ✅ / ⚠️ / 🔴>

📦 COMMITS (<N> total)
  <hash> — <message>
  ...

⬡ KNOWLEDGE MESH
  Milestone node: <node id> — <name>
  Embedding: ✅ / ⚠️

📖 README.md
  Versão: <old> → <new>
  Badge neural_mesh: <old> → <new> nós
  Seção adicionada: <section name>

🧠 HANDOFF.md
  Fase selada: <phase name>
  Seções atualizadas: Task State, Record, Pending Actions, GraphRAG State

🚀 PUSH
  Branch: main → origin/main
  Commits enviados: <N>

📌 SPRINT
  sprint-priorities.md: <atualizado ✅ / sem mudanças>
  scratch/ cleanup: <arquivos movidos ✅ / limpo ✅>
  MCP docs: <ok ✅ / pendência: X>

⏭  PRÓXIMOS PASSOS
  1. <top pending action>
  2. <second pending action>
  3. <third pending action>

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  Assinado: Claude Code — Braço de Engenharia do AIOS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```

**Invariants — never skip:**
- Security audit must run and report before any commit (Phase 1)
- **Phase 3 (milestone node) must run BEFORE Phase 4 (README badge query)** — ensures count includes the handoff node
- **Phase 5b secret scan must run on HANDOFF.md before its commit** — no exceptions
- **Documentation must NEVER contain actual credential values** — only describe where they live
- README.md badge counts must reflect real DB state (query live, after milestone insert)
- HANDOFF.md must be updated even if nothing changed (session note)
- Push must happen — remote must always mirror local main
- Knowledge Mesh milestone must be attempted (failures are logged, not blocking)
- git pre-commit hook (`.git/hooks/pre-commit`) is the last barrier — do not bypass with `--no-verify`
